## Previous cycle (hundred-and-sixty-eighth research cycle)

A **construction-and-decision cycle that moves one score.** The cycle's five priorities were executed: every total re-derived and every item 16 audited in all three places; **CRYPTO-EDGE-001's marker rule was found to be stated twice in two incompatible ways** - the rule clause says "five times the contract's trailing **30-day** median" and the measurement clause says "five times its trailing **30-observation** median" - and only the 30-observation reading reproduces the entry's own recorded figures, so the rule is corrected in place and the marker's bite is then measured at the plan's binding width on the **specified** instrument, where it is **total** (57 of 57 elevated and 12 of 12 depressed trigger days excluded, leaving zero surviving trigger days) because 263 of the 330 live-universe contracts mark at least once and the broad reading saturates; **three recorded figures across the two funding and premium entries were found not to reproduce and are superseded in place**, and the full marker-width table is recorded rather than three points on it; **CRYPTO-EDGE-004's bounded free-data question is answered on measurement** - the community tier's own market-capitalisation series carries the state variable over **all 2,850** of the panel's dates - so the free-data score moves **4/5 -> 5/5** and the entry's total moves **48 -> 51**; **CRYPTO-EDGE-003's declared window re-derives exactly** from the plan's own text on a fresh pull of both legs, the plan's matching-rule text and its own matching count are found to disagree by one settlement that moves no derived count, and the extension's condition is re-tested and remains unmet; all four totals re-derived (**001 = 58, 002 = 46 rejected, 003 = 55, 004 = 51**); rank order 001 > 003 > 004 by **3** and **4** points; all confidence low; version stamps now 001 -> v1.20, 003 -> v1.17, 004 -> v1.17; the three carried disagreements re-tested, with **CRYPTO-EDGE-004's free-data disagreement resolved in favour of 5/5** on the measurement that supersedes the recorded reason for 4/5.

**Key findings:**

1. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the plan's contemporaneity marker is specified two different ways, the entry's own recorded figures select one of them, and the rule is corrected in place.** The rule clause (v1.14) says "five times the contract's trailing **30-day** median daily forced-liquidation count"; the fifty-third cycle's measurement clause says "five times its trailing **30-observation** median of daily counts, the current observation excluded from its own median". These are different objects and they are not close: a calendar-day window counts an absent per-event file as a missing day, while an observation window counts it out of the history altogether, so for a thinly covered contract the observation window reaches further back and its baseline is materially different. On a fresh pull of both arms' per-event records (linear **64,820** symbol-day archives, inverse **17,182**) and an implementation written from the plan's text alone, **only the 30-observation reading reproduces the recorded figures exactly**: at the pre-registered 5x the inverse arm gives **83** marked days at the eight-contract width and **152** at the live-universe width, elevated exclusions **26 of 53** (leaving **27**) and **45 of 61** (leaving **16**), and the sensitivity **2x -> 2, 3x -> 6, 5x -> 16, 10x -> 39, 20x -> 57** surviving elevated trigger days - every one of them the recorded value. The 30-calendar-day reading gives **78** and **164** marked days and **22** and **42** elevated exclusions and reproduces none of them; the calendar reading with an absent file counted as a zero-event day gives **130** and **267** marked days and **29** and **53** exclusions. **This is the same class of defect the fifty-first cycle found in the independent-window rule and the fifty-fourth cycle found in the matching rule: a rule stated twice in two incompatible ways, where the recorded numbers silently select one of them.**

2. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - at the plan's binding width on the specified instrument, the marker's bite is total, and the mechanism is a fact about the cross-section.** On the linear arm (2023-06-25 to 2024-03-31) with the marker computed over every live-universe contract carrying a per-event file - **330 contracts**, **63,862 of 67,516** live-universe symbol-days carrying a file (**94.6%**) against the inverse arm's **13,909 of 19,757** (**70.4%**) - **250 of the 281** arm days are marked at 5x, and the marker excludes **57 of the 57** elevated trigger days and **12 of the 12** depressed trigger days, leaving **zero** surviving trigger days on either arm of the state variable. The calendar-day reading gives **251** marked days and the same **57 of 57**. **263 of the 330** live-universe contracts mark at least once, and the contracts contributing most of the marks are small ones (**TUSDT** 40 marked days, **1000FLOKIUSDT** 37, **ZRXUSDT** 36, **SPELLUSDT** 36, **MDTUSDT** 36, **ICXUSDT** 36, **LPTUSDT** 35, **API3USDT** 35) whose trailing median daily forced-liquidation count is one or two events, so a 5x threshold is crossed routinely. The plan's own required strict count is reported beside it: at the linear arm's live-universe width **5** marked days and **0 of 57** elevated trigger days excluded (all 57 survive), against the inverse arm's live-universe **14** marked days and **8 of 61** excluded (53 survive) and its eight-contract width's **6** marked days and **3 of 53** excluded. **The entry's effective sample on its specified instrument is therefore either zero or 57 according to which of the plan's two declared readings is used, and the plan declares the broad reading binding. The decision is not taken in this cycle - no outcome has been read - and it is handed to the next cycle as a dated question with its numbers attached.**

3. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - three recorded figures do not reproduce and are superseded in place, and the coverage rule's treatment is declared and measured.** The fifty-sixth cycle's inverse-arm depressed-side exclusions (**45 of the 54** depressed trigger days at the live-universe width, leaving 9, and **14** at the eight-contract width) re-derive as **20 of 54** (leaving **34**) and **8 of 48** (leaving **40**); the v1.16 clause's four-contract broad triple (**38** marked, **12** excluded, **43** surviving) re-derives as **51** marked, **25** excluded, **30** surviving, and does not reproduce under either of the other two window readings either; and the v1.16 strict triple (**7**, **14**, **11**) re-derives as **4**, **3**, **8**. The elevated side reproduces to the digit at the eight-contract and live-universe widths. Separately, the **coverage rule's treatment was declared in writing before these counts were read** - **flag and retain** - and its effect is now measured: on the inverse arm at the live-universe width **25 of the 61** elevated trigger days and **18 of the 54** depressed trigger days fall below the arm's median coverage of **64.3%**, and excluding them instead would cut the surviving elevated sample from **16** to **11** and the depressed from **34** to **23**. **A cross-sectional median is computable on a depleted and date-clustered cross-section, but its estimand is conditional**: the plan now requires the coverage to be reported beside every count, requires the coverage's exogeneity to the premium to be established rather than assumed, and requires the trigger band to be computed on the same observable the trigger is read from.

4. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the declared window re-derives exactly, the plan's matching-rule text and the plan's own matching count disagree by one settlement, and the disagreement moves no derived count.** The publisher's leg was re-pulled (81 monthly files per instrument, 2020-01 to 2026-09, **7,395** settlements per instrument, maximum timestamp jitter **47 ms**) and the second venue's leg from its all-swap daily product (first file 2022-01-17, last 2026-10-21, **5,216** rows per instrument) with its legacy per-instrument product covering 2022-01-01 to 2022-01-16 (**96** rows). The window's **5,202** grid instants re-derive; the trigger settlements are **997**/**993** with the same tail splits; the episodes **413**/**408**; the independent windows **238**/**243** at 5 days and **76**/**74** at 20 days; and on the slow clock **247** weekly samples, **235** eligible, **77**/**90** extreme weeks and **46**/**49** independent 20-day windows. The fifty-third cycle's short-window figures reproduce exactly (**4,037** matched, **191** weekly samples, **60**/**64** extreme weeks, **36**/**36** independent 20-day windows), which is the check that the implementation is the entry's own and not a new one. The rule as written (half-open 8-hour bucket) gives **5,201** matched and the plan's recorded **5,200** is what the tolerance reading gives; the two readings produce **identical derived counts**, so the fifty-fourth cycle's "upper bounds by one settlement" caveat is **closed**. Two figures in the cycle's own text are corrected in place: the second venue's row count is **5,216 per instrument** (10,432 across the two), and the off-grid **2022-12-18 18:54:00** settlement's **ETH** rate is **+0.0000367080042939** (BTC **-0.000012515715975** confirmed), both re-verified live against the venue's two products.

5. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 - the bounded free-data question is answered on measurement, the free-data score moves 4/5 -> 5/5, and the total moves 48 -> 51.** The community tier's own market-capitalisation series is present on **every one of the panel's 2,850 dates** (2019-01-01 to 2026-10-20): per-date asset counts of **minimum 1, median 426, maximum 746** for the estimated metric, **minimum 71, median 112, maximum 118** for the current metric and **minimum 121, median 499, maximum 997** for the price. The state variable's joint requirement - market capitalisation today, price today and price 30 days earlier - is met on **every** date, with a per-date count of **minimum 19, median 452, maximum 772** and **0 of the 2,820** eligible dates below ten assets. The census is verified against realised pulls rather than the catalogue alone: **484 assets** pulled, **446** covering their entire declared window, **38** carrying gaps totalling **51** interior gap cells. The route is **keyless** (HTTP 200 without a credential) and throttled (HTTP 403 under bursts). **The fifty-first cycle's recorded reason for 4/5 - that the free market-capitalisation route covers only 366 of the panel's 2,850 dates - is superseded in place**, every input the registered test needs is now free, dated and available across the whole window, and the remaining caveats (the estimated-supply definition rather than the published screen, stratum Jaccard **0.048**, a replication rather than an exact replication, a reported rather than fixed cross-section) are **fidelity** caveats belonging to the robustness dimension, where the entry already sits at its floor. **The registered statistic is now specified on that panel** - the difference of cross-sectional spreads between the high- and low-dispersion states, on the venue's USDⓈ-M perpetual daily klines, with the price source declared per contract and cross-checked on the overlap, the size label taken from `CapMrktEstUSD` at the $60.3m reference ceiling with `CapMrktCurUSD` as the pre-registered robustness check, a date usable only with a price on the date and 30 days earlier, and a mandatory coverage report beside every count - **so that the statistic is executable without the unread paper, whose read stays closed**.

6. **Audit - one score moved on a measured access fact, and four plan-of-record defects were repaired in place.** All four totals re-derived from their seven dimension contributions: **001 = 58** (16+6+3+15+6+8+4), **002 = 46** (rejected; 12+3+3+15+3+6+4), **003 = 55** (12+3+6+15+3+8+8), **004 = 51** (12+6+3+15+3+8+4); rank order unchanged at 001 > 003 > 004 by **3** and **4** points; all confidence low. Item 16 audited in all three places for all four entries with no stale score remaining; the item-3 status blocks state the current total, confidence and version stamp (001: 58/low/v1.20; 002: 46/rejected/low/v1.6; 003: 55/low/v1.17; 004: **51**/low/v1.17). The item-14 clauses were audited for internal consistency and four defects were repaired in place: a clause-number collision created by this cycle's insertion in CRYPTO-EDGE-003, a mis-count in its second-venue row count, a sign error in its off-grid ETH rate, and an un-attributed hole in CRYPTO-EDGE-001's zero-close clause (the 2023-08 hole is the **inverse** family's; the linear family's monthly product has no interior hole across the arm). The three carried disagreements were re-tested on their own terms: **001 falsifiability 4/5** upheld, **003 free-data 5/5** upheld, and **004 free-data resolved in favour of 5/5** on the measurement that supersedes the recorded reason for 4/5. The claim-side search returned **no** new peer-reviewed test of dispersion-as-state against Bitcoin-volatility-as-state; the public research ledger was re-read and is **unchanged** (40 documents, the latest still `72-point-in-time-carry.md`, `pushed_at` still 2026-08-28T00:26:56Z).

### New this cycle

- **One score moved, on evidence.** CRYPTO-EDGE-004's free-data accessibility moves **4/5 -> 5/5** and the entry's total **48 -> 51**, because the community tier's own market-capitalisation series is measured to cover **every one of the panel's 2,850 dates** with the state variable's joint requirement met on every date; the recorded reason for 4/5 is superseded in place. The rank order is unchanged (001 > 003 > 004) and the margin over CRYPTO-EDGE-003 narrows from 7 to 4 points because of this move and nothing else.
- **One rule is corrected in place.** CRYPTO-EDGE-001's contemporaneity marker's trailing window is corrected from "30-day" to "30-observation" with the current observation excluded from its own median, because only that reading reproduces the entry's own recorded figures.
- **Three recorded figures are superseded in place.** CRYPTO-EDGE-001's inverse-arm depressed-side exclusion pair, its v1.16 four-contract broad triple and its v1.16 strict triple do not reproduce under the corrected marker definition, and the re-derived values replace them.
- **One declared choice is now measured rather than assumed.** CRYPTO-EDGE-001's coverage treatment (flag and retain) was declared in writing before the counts were read, and the alternative (exclude below-median-coverage trigger days) is reported as a sensitivity worth about a third of the surviving sample.
- **One named question is handed on rather than answered.** Which reading of the marker's cross-section binds for CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - broad (zero surviving trigger days on the specified instrument at the live-universe width) or strict (57 surviving) - must be fixed in the plan of record **before any outcome is read**, and it is not taken in this cycle.
- **No hypothesis was added, and no outcome statistic was computed.** The cycle's output is counts, coverage ratios, dates, timestamps, access statuses and specification work; no return, spread, drawdown, hit rate, win rate, profit factor, Sharpe ratio or forward relationship was computed for any strategy or any registered prediction.

## Previous cycle (fifty-sixth research cycle)

A **measurement-and-decision cycle.** The cycle's five priorities were executed: every total re-derived and every item 16 audited in all three places; **CRYPTO-EDGE-001's primary arm measured** — the record's statement that the linear arm's effective sample "remains unmeasured" is superseded in place, because the relocated USDⓈ-M per-event record is free, dated and complete for the whole 281-day window and the plan's trigger construction applies to the linear live-perpetual universe, giving 57 elevated and 12 depressed trigger days at the live-universe width, 59 and 18 at the eight-contract width, and 37 surviving elevated trigger days after the pre-registered 5x marker excludes 20 of 57 (35.1%); the marker's bite on the inverse arm is re-measured at the plan's own width and reproduces 45 of 61 excluded (73.8%) leaving 16, exactly the recorded binding count; and the **premium panel's cross-section coverage is measured and becomes a reported quantity**, because the zero-close defect the catalogue recorded as 334 cells across eight contracts is family-wide — 35.4% of the inverse family's monthly 1-day rows and 26.9% of the linear family's — so the "live universe" is 11 to 41 usable observations on a population of 41 to 45 contracts; **CRYPTO-EDGE-003's window extension priced and declined** — the declared window's 5,202 grid instants re-derive exactly, the extension's ceiling is the publisher's next monthly file at 93 instants (1.79%), the second venue's leg has advanced a day and covers 63 of them (1.21%), the publisher's leg contributes 0 because its 2026-10 file is not published, and the declared window is therefore **kept as the binding reading** with the extension registered as a priced, declared option under a stated rule; **CRYPTO-EDGE-004's paper-version search exhausted** — three fresh searches and the publisher's own endpoints leave the regression unreadable on free data, so the read is closed rather than deferred, and the registered source's own authors' later paper (SSRN 7105658) is registered as a claim-side source; all four totals re-derived and confirmed unchanged (001 = 58, 002 = 46 rejected, 003 = 55, 004 = 48); rank order 001 > 003 > 004 by 3 and 7 points; all confidence low; item 16 audited in all three places for all four entries with no stale score remaining; item-3 status blocks state current totals and confidence with version stamps (001: 58/low/v1.19; 002: 46/rejected/low/v1.6; 003: 55/low/v1.16; 004: 48/low/v1.16) and their stale cycle labels repaired in place; carried disagreements upheld (001 falsifiability 4/5, 003 free-data 5/5, 004 free-data 4/5); claim-side search executed, one new source; public research ledger re-read and unchanged (40 documents, latest `72-point-in-time-carry.md`, `pushed_at` 2026-08-28T00:26:56Z). **No hypothesis added and no score moved.**

**Key findings:**

1. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the primary arm's effective sample is measured, and the unreachable part is now bounded to a date.** The fifty-fifth cycle recorded that the specified linear arm has no free per-event record after 2024-03-31 and concluded that "the primary arm's effective sample remains unmeasured". The first half is right; the inference is withdrawn. The relocated product at `data3/liquidationSnapshot/` carries the whole 281-day window for the eight family-analogue contracts (BTCUSDT 281/281 files and 326,320 unique forced-liquidation events, ETHUSDT 281/281 and 197,333, SOLUSDT 281/281 and 236,483, DOGEUSDT 281/281 and 110,102, LINKUSDT 281/281 and 100,574, ADAUSDT 281/281 and 89,815, BNBUSDT 280/281 and 79,213, XRPUSDT 280/281 and 117,892, de-duplicated on the full row), and the v1.18 trigger construction applies to the linear live-perpetual universe by the entry's own standing rule. The linear arm's effective sample is therefore **measured for 2023-06-25 to 2024-03-31** and unmeasured only after 2024-03-31 — a bounded statement, which is a materially better position than an open one. The marker's bite is also **lower than the inverse arm's at the same width** (35.1% against the inverse arm's 49.1% at eight contracts, and against 73.8% at the live universe), which is a fact about the two families' event densities and not a choice. The entry can therefore still advance as a shortlisted candidate, but its binding effective sample must now be stated **per statistic**: the trigger and the price-based tail statistics are free over the whole price history, and the per-event liquidation-share statistic is free to 2024-03-31 on the specified instrument.

2. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the cross-section is not the live universe, and its coverage is now a reported quantity.** The zero-close defect the catalogue recorded as **334** cells across the eight family-analogue contracts is **family-wide**: measured at family scale on a fresh pull, **7,559 of 21,342** inverse monthly 1-day rows (**35.4%**) and **19,660 of 73,110** linear rows (**26.9%**) carry a literal zero close, most of them with the bar's open, high and low populated, so the recorded 334 is a lower bound on a defect reaching a third of the inverse family's observations. The record's "41 to 45 perpetuals on every one of the 478 arm days" is a true statement about the **contract population** and a false one about the **observations the cross-sectional median is actually taken on**, which are **11 to 41** per day, a median coverage of **64.3%** and a minimum of **26.8%** (2023-10-23, where 30 of 41 contracts show a zero close); the linear arm runs 207-282 contracts against 105-270 usable observations, a median coverage of **72.2%** and a minimum of **46.7%** (2024-01-29). The depletion clusters in dates, so a trigger day's cross-section is a different population on a low-coverage day than on a high-coverage one. The missing-value rule is unchanged; what changes is that **a validator must report the day's coverage beside every trigger count** and flag any trigger day below the arm's median coverage. The **2023-08** monthly hole is also shown to be family-wide (**47** symbol-months, with the daily product filling 164 of the arm's 213 hole cells), not confined to the four contracts the catalogue sampled.

3. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the window extension is priced to the instant and declined, and the declared window stands.** The declared window [2022-01-01 00:00 UTC, 2026-09-30 16:00 UTC] contains **5,202** 8-hour grid instants, re-derived independently and matching the recorded publisher-instant count exactly. The extension's **ceiling** is fixed by the publisher's next monthly file: **93** grid instants, **1.79%** of the window, and no more. ## Previous cycle (fifty-fourth research cycle, 2026-10-22)

The fifty-fourth research cycle is complete. All four totals re-derived from their seven dimension contributions and confirmed unchanged: **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 = 58** (16+6+3+15+6+8+4), v1.17; **CRYPTO-EDGE-002 = 46** (rejected; 12+3+3+15+3+6+4), v1.6; **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 = 55** (12+3+6+15+3+8+8), v1.15; **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 = 48** (12+6+3+12+3+8+4), v1.15. Rank order 001 > 003 > 004 by 3 and 7 points; all confidence low. No hypothesis added; no score moved; no empirical validation performed; no return, spread, drawdown, hit rate, win rate, Sharpe ratio or strategy-performance quantity computed for any strategy.

**Priority 1 - CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - trigger executable from its own text.** The trigger's construction is written into item 14 (v1.18): the daily cross-sectional median of premium-index 1-day closes across the live inverse-perpetual universe on each day; a trailing 90-day band with linear-interpolated percentiles; the band includes the current observation; an elevated trigger day is one on which the median is at or above its trailing 90th percentile, a depressed trigger day at or below its trailing 10th percentile; the day set is calendar days on which the live universe carries an observation; a zero or missing close is a missing value and not a zero premium; marker evaluation begins on day 31 of the arm. Under this construction the trigger counts reproduce as 53/48 (superseding the fifty-second cycle's 56/54), the marker side reproduces exactly at 83 marked days at 5x, and at the live-universe width the marker leaves 16 surviving elevated trigger days out of 61 (73.8% excluded), 27 at the eight-contract width. A validator must report the trigger counts after the marker.

**Priority 2 - CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - counts re-derived under the matching rule now written, and window extension priced.** The matching rule in item 14 (v1.15) gives the second venue **5,200 of 5,202** publisher instants inside the declared window [2022-01-01 00:00 UTC, 2026-09-30 16:00 UTC]. Under this rule the derived counts (4,931 eligible, 997/993 trigger settlements, 413/408 episodes, 238/243 and 76/74 independent windows) were measured on a reading one settlement larger (the bucket-join reading of 5,201); under the stated rule they are upper bounds by one settlement per arm. The genuinely absent instant 2022-02-25 08:00 falls in both arms (it is a publisher grid instant missing from the second venue's coverage on both BTC and ETH). The cost of the extension the publisher's 2026-10 monthly file will bring - additional matched settlements, eligibility rate, trigger counts and independent windows beyond the declared end - is recorded as a **cost, not a result**, so that a later extension is a declared choice with a price attached rather than a silent drift.

**Priority 3 - CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the third-venue question, now asked.** Compared the plan's pair (publisher's monthly per-symbol funding archive against the second venue's all-swap daily product) with a third venue's funding on the same settlements (Hyperliquid's public info API, free and dated from 2023-05-11, a USDC-margined decentralised venue whose instruments are not comparable to the entry's specified USDT-margined centralised perpetuals). This is a construction and coverage fact, not a return quantity, so it is inside this repository's scope: the third venue does not extend the entry's constructible pair because its instrument family and margining differ, and its funding history starts in 2023 rather than 2021, so it cannot cover the entry's full window. The entry remains a two-venue construction, and its sign remains an estimable parameter, not an assumption.

**Priority 4 - CRYPTO-EDGE-004 - citation set read, market-capitalisation proxy settled.** The paper's citation set (Bailey 2014 deflated Sharpe; Barroso-Santa-Clara 2015; Moreira-Muir 2017; Fieberg 2025; Liu-Tsyvinski 2021; Babiak 2025; Daniel-Hirshleifer 2016; plus the non-peer-reviewed working paper for the correlation control) does not settle the paper's two placebo constructs or its monthly holding, and the paper's regression itself remains unread (only the publisher's HTTP-403 server serves it). The free trailing-365-day market-capitalisation route covers **366 of the panel's 2,850 dates** (the trailing year, 2025-10-21 to 2026-10-20); the remaining **2,484 dates** (87.1%) are behind the reference screen. Decision: the plan uses the free trailing-365-day route for the panel's final year and states that the entry's replication is paid for beyond it - no alternative free proxy was found. The free-data accessibility score stays at 4/5 with the corrected reason.

**Priority 5 - Audit - item 16, item 3, item 14 and the version stamps.** All four totals re-derived and confirmed unchanged. Item 16 audited in all three places for all four entries - no stale score remains. Every entry's item-3 status block states its current total and confidence with a version stamp (001: 58/low/v1.17; 002: 46/rejected/low/v1.6; 003: 55/low/v1.15; 004: 48/low/v1.15). Each entry's item-14 plan of record is internally consistent and consistent with items 15, 16 and 17; each heading carries the item-14 stamp (001 v1.17, 002 v1.6, 003 v1.15, 004 v1.15), which is authoritative. The current ranking table - with each entry's next research step - is written into the top section of RANKED_SHORTLIST.md.

**Artefacts updated:** all five research artefacts and both handoff files. Research only; no performance statistics computed.

## Previous cycle (fifty-second research cycle)

A **re-derivation-and-audit cycle.** The cycle's five priorities were executed and all four totals were re-derived and confirmed unchanged (001 = 58, 002 = 46 rejected, 003 = 55, 004 = 48; rank order unchanged at 001 > 003 > 004 by 3 and 7; all confidences low). Its content is recorded here for the first time: the fifty-second cycle wrote only into IDEA_LEDGER.md, so this section, the SOURCE_NOTES section, the PROCESS_CHANGELOG entries and the REJECTED_AND_DEFERRED section for that cycle are back-filled by the fifty-third cycle. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001:** the contemporaneity marker's bite was measured on the arm the entry is reported on - the inverse family, 2023-06-25 to 2024-10-14, 478 calendar days - with the marker's cross-section and left-edge rule declared, and the recorded figures were 56 elevated and 54 depressed trigger days in 32 and 33 episodes, 83 marked days across the cross-section at the pre-registered 5x, elevated-arm exclusions of 28 at 5x (16 under the strict reading) and depressed-arm exclusions of 7, with the marker's sensitivity to its constant quantified; the prospective free stream route was re-tested, with `stream.binance.com:9443` answering HTTP 451 from this machine and `fstream.binance.com` and `data-stream.binance.vision` answering HTTP 101. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003:** the plan of record was completed where the fifty-first cycle left it open - the net-of-carry accrual rule made executable, the block structure fixed, the null clause completed - a fifth unstated convention (the slow clock's left edge) was found, the legacy product's row structure was corrected in place, the legacy vintage's whole count set was measured and found **vintage-stable**, and an independent implementation on an independent pull reproduced the recorded counts to the digit, including the four minimum-detectable-effect multipliers (2.8169, 2.8504, 2.8523, 2.8821). **CRYPTO-EDGE-004:** the paper's own result was reached by a dated third-party route (Sharpe 0.63 to 0.80, maximum drawdown -42.5% to -17.1%), the trailing-365-day free market-capitalisation route was used and covers 366 of the panel's 2,850 dates, and the keyless tier's rate-limit behaviour was measured. No hypothesis was added, no score moved, and no empirical validation was performed.


## Previous cycle (fifty-first research cycle)

A **re-derivation-and-decision cycle.** The cycle's five priorities were executed: every total re-derived and every item 16 audited in all three places; **CRYPTO-EDGE-001's falsifiability score decided and its reverse-causality concern discharged** with a pre-registered operational exclusion; **CRYPTO-EDGE-003's specification re-derived from its own text by an independent implementation on a fresh pull** of both legs, with the parts that do not reproduce superseded in place and the minimum detectable effect written in as a closed form; **CRYPTO-EDGE-004's own published source attacked on every open route**, its read completed as far as access allows, and the free-data accessibility score decided at 4/5 with a corrected reason; and the fifth priority in full - the **adversarial re-test of the three carried disagreements** plus a **dated claim-side search** across the three registered predictions. All four totals are **unchanged** (001 = 58, 002 = 46 rejected, 003 = 55, 004 = 48) and **no hypothesis was added**. The cycle's real work was to ask whether the catalogue's own specifications are executable by a third party, and for the entry that had claimed they were, the answer is **no**.

**Key findings:**

1. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - an independent implementation written from the plan of record's text reproduces the entry's arithmetic exactly on everything the text fixes.** Both legs were pulled again - the publisher's monthly per-symbol `fundingRate` archive (**162** files, **7,395** settlements per symbol, every row's `funding_interval_hours` equal to **8**, **no** two settlements in one 8-hour grid bucket, jitter at most **47 ms**, no duplicate `calc_time`) and the second venue's **legacy** per-instrument daily product (**381** files per instrument) plus its **all-swap** daily tree to **2026-10-20** - and the plan's legacy-leg offset was **confirmed by measurement rather than assumed**: the legacy `real_funding_rate` at `funding_time` T equals the all-swap `funding_rate` at settlement instant **T minus 8 hours** on **93 of 93** compared instants with **0** mismatches, the forecast column mismatching on **93 of 93**. Under the plan's own rules this reproduces **4,037** matched settlements in the window, **3,767** eligible, **738** trigger settlements for BTC (**348** upper, **390** lower) and **734** for ETH (**338** upper, **396** lower), **310** and **303** merged episodes, **180** independent 5-day windows for both instruments and **58** and **56** independent 20-day windows - **to the digit**.

2. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the specification is not executable as written, in three specific places, and all three are now fixed in the plan of record.** (i) **The window's end was never stated**, and every recorded count is conditional on it: the window is **2022-01-01 00:00 to 2025-09-08 00:00 UTC (exclusive)**, the last matched settlement being 2025-09-07 16:00, which is where the second venue's **legacy** product stops being published - the **all-swap** product has continued to **2026-10-20**, so the recorded window is a **pull boundary and not a pre-registered date**; extending it to the end of the data gives **5,201** in-window matched settlements, **4,931** eligible, **997**/**993** triggers, **413**/**408** episodes and **76**/**74** independent 20-day windows, about **+30%** on the binding number. (ii) **The independent-window rule is wrong as written**: read literally over the eligible settlement series, the v1.11 clause (g) yields **270** windows at 5 days and **68** at 20 days - **50% and 17% more** than the recorded **180** and **58**/**56** on which the effective-sample bound and the minimum detectable effect are built; the recorded numbers reproduce under one rule only - **each trigger settlement opens a forward window, and the next opens at the first trigger at or after the previous window's end**. (iii) **The slow clock's four unstated conventions** (the daily aggregation, the trailing window's alignment, the sampling phase and the band's include/exclude rule) are left open, and across **96** combinations of them **no combination produces the recorded BTC (57 extreme weeks, 36 windows) and ETH (65, 39) together**; with the conventions fixed, the window gives **191** weekly samples, **60** and **64** extreme weeks and **36 independent 20-day windows for both instruments** - so the recorded ETH-against-BTC difference of **39 against 36** does not survive a single declared convention, and what genuinely differs between the instruments is the extreme-week count.

3. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - four of the catalogue's own recorded numbers are superseded in place, and one of them is impossible as written.** The pair "**5,201 matched settlements per instrument (4,037 inside the window)**" cannot both hold on one pull: the measured structure is **6,297** matched in total, **1,096** before the window start, **4,037** inside the window to 2025-09-08, and **5,201** inside it if the window is extended to the end of the data - the two recorded numbers are counts on **two different pulls**. "**761 pre-window matched settlements**" does not reproduce: the measured number is **1,096**. The alternative reading's numbers do not reproduce either: the measured figures are **903**/**901** trigger settlements, **385**/**375** episodes and **225**/**223** and **74**/**71** independent windows, against the recorded **779**/**775**, **333**/**325** and "**192 and 62** independent 20-day windows" - a pair that is internally impossible, since **192** non-overlapping 20-day windows cannot fit in a 1,346-day window, the maximum being **67**.

4. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the minimum detectable effect the thirty-sixth cycle required is now in the plan of record, in the only form this repository may produce it.** **MDE = (t_{1-alpha/2,n-1} + t_{1-beta,n-1}) x SE_block**, two-sided **alpha = 0.05**, **power = 0.80**, the multiplier taken from the binding window counts - **2.8169** at n = 180, **2.8504** at n = 58, **2.8523** at n = 56 and **2.8821** at n = 36 - where **SE_block** is the block-bootstrap standard error of the registered statistic in bp per week, which the **validator** measures. The catalogue does not estimate SE_block, because estimating it would mean measuring the outcome series, which is empirical validation and outside this repository's scope. The block structure is **completed, not replaced**: the registered forward window itself is the block on the instantaneous construction, and a **moving block of at least four weekly observations (28 days)** on the slow clock, with the sensitivity to 2, 4 and 8 blocks reported. One new construction requirement comes from the claim side: the registered statistic must be **net of the funding differential accrued over the forward window**, because the cross-venue funding spread is a documented carry (about 7% annualised on the venue studied) and the entry's two venues are *defined* by their funding rates.

5. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the falsifiability score is decided and held at 4/5, and the reverse-causality concern is discharged operationally.** The 3/5 placement carried from the forty-ninth cycle is **declined, with its reasoning written into the plan of record**: its premise (an unbounded pooled ratio) no longer describes the entry, because the registered statistic is read on its bounded region - the daily cross-sectional median, **0.0044% to 2.9873%** - and its second premise (a removed transport instrument) describes a **failed pre-registered test** (T1, FAIL on both arms) rather than a lost instrument. The score is not moved up either, because the entry's outcome variable is a return statistic this repository may not compute. The operational discharge is new: **the contemporaneous-crash marker is the venue's own forced-liquidation count above five times the contract's trailing 30-day median daily count, evaluated on the settlement day and the preceding day; a trigger observation dated on a marked day, or within 24 hours after one, is excluded and the exclusion count is reported** - free, dated, computed from forced liquidations rather than returns, and fixed before any outcome is measured. The source that motivates it has been **revised into an empirical result**: the reverse-causality reference is now a three-author study whose evidence from **200 Binance USDT-margined perpetuals** finds that "**basis reversals are associated with directional liquidation**" (He, Wang and Zhang, 30 August 2026), and a separate minute-level study of the 10 October 2025 cascade documents that **futures led the crash**, the basis swinging **$1,367 from -$534 to +$833 in eight minutes**.

6. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the prospective free stream route is re-tested: the host on file is blocked from this machine and two alternatives answer.** `stream.binance.com:9443` answers **HTTP 451** ("Service unavailable from a restricted location"), while `fstream.binance.com` and `data-stream.binance.vision` both answer the identical WebSocket upgrade with **HTTP 101 Switching Protocols**. A capture can therefore be continued or restarted against them; the capture's own contents remain outside the repository's evidence, and the entry's retrospective coverage is untouched.

7. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 - the entry's own published source is still not obtainable, but the read is now complete as far as access allows, and the free-data score is decided at 4/5 with a corrected reason.** SSRN 6648082 answers **HTTP 403** with a Cloudflare interstitial on the abstract page, the PDF delivery endpoint and the DOI redirect, so the full text stays inaccessible and the source stays **access 2**; but the **complete abstract and the complete twelve-item reference list** are now on file, free and dated, from **Crossref's deposited metadata** (DOI 10.2139/ssrn.6648082, deposited 2026-04-27), with **OpenAlex** indexing the metadata and not the text (`has_content: false`, null `pdf_url`), **Semantic Scholar** holding **no record** (HTTP 404) and **ResearchGate** offering the file on request only. Read whole, the abstract is **closer to this entry's registered prediction than the record's summary of it** - the same upper-tail nonlinearity, the same two controls (Bitcoin realised volatility and average cross-asset correlation) and the same direction - but the state variable is **lagged and orthogonalised**, the statistic is a **horse-race regression** rather than the entry's registered **difference of spreads**, and the universe is the construct this entry *replicates* rather than the one it claims; so a confirmation is written into the plan of record and **no score moves**. On accessibility the free tier is **narrower than the record stated**: the historical market-capitalisation endpoints answer **HTTP 401** with error code 10012 - "Public API users are limited to querying historical data within the past 365 days" - and a query **inside** that trailing window answers **HTTP 200** with a `market_data.market_cap` block, so contemporaneous market capitalisation **is** free and dated for a trailing year and paid for anything older; that covers **366** of the panel's **2,850** dates, and the community panel remains revisable, so the score stays at **4/5** with the reason corrected. The keyless screen's rule is tightened against the rate limit, which answered **HTTP 429** on two of five page requests today, because a truncated screen changes the denominator of the capitalisation ratio the rule exists to police.

8. **Audit and adversarial.** All four totals re-derived from their seven dimension contributions and confirmed unchanged - **001 = 58** (16+6+3+15+6+8+4), **002 = 46** (rejected; 12+3+3+15+3+6+4), **003 = 55** (12+3+6+15+3+8+8), **004 = 48** (12+6+3+12+3+8+4). Item 16 was audited in all three places for all four entries and no stale score remains. The three carried disagreements were re-tested on their own terms: **001 falsifiability is decided at 4/5** (the carried 3/5 declined, with the reasoning in the plan of record), **003 free-data is upheld at 5/5** (both legs are free and dated and were re-pulled this cycle, the publisher's leg with every file's `funding_interval_hours` equal to 8), and **004 free-data is decided at 4/5** with the reason corrected. One arithmetic defect in the record was found and **repaired in place**: CRYPTO-EDGE-004's v1.11 clause (ii) printed the exit decomposition as "5,292 / 5,740 / 194" and "5,337 / 5,783 / 194", placing the **entries** counts in the upward-migration slot; the correct decompositions are **5,292 exits / 5,098 upward / 194 panel deaths** and **5,337 exits / 5,143 upward / 194 panel deaths, with 5,783 entries**. The public research ledger was re-read and is **unchanged** - **40** hypothesis documents, the latest still `72-point-in-time-carry.md`, `pushed_at` still 2026-08-28.

**Current ranking (totals re-derived from the seven dimension contributions):**

| Rank | ID | Thesis | Total score | Confidence | Main mechanism | Decisive advantage | Largest uncertainty | Free-data feasibility | Next research step |
|------|-----|--------|-------------|------------|----------------|--------------------|---------------------|-----------------------|--------------------|
| 1 | CRYPTO-EDGE-001 | Extreme perp basis predicts elevated crash frequency and severity, not negative mean returns, over 1-4 weeks, with a separate registered arm for the basis-compression state | 58/100 | Low | Constrained cash-and-carry arbitrage plus trend-chasing leveraged retail demand; forced sell unwind; a volatility and instability channel rather than a directional one | The only entry with peer-reviewed support for a *tail* claim, and the mandatory comparator for every other entry | The record's window still ends **2024-03-31** (2024-10-14 inverse), so the post-July-2024 regime is uncovered; the transport instrument has been removed from the plan of record; the registered statistic is an unbounded ratio in its pooled form (maximum 35,442% on a contract-day with $10 of opening open interest value); the pre-registered second-venue transport (T2) is unexecutable on free data; and **the reverse-causality direction is now supported by an empirical source** - "basis reversals are associated with directional liquidation" on 200 Binance perpetuals - so the entry's compression arm needs its exclusion, which this cycle wrote but whose bite is unmeasured | Fully free, and the census re-verifies it at panel scale: the linear archive's **129,640 objects** reproduce the recorded **8,218,185 unique events** and the 61.95% SELL share to the digit; the free stream capture remains a **non-substitute** (no time overlap with the archive product, licence terms unresolved), and the host it depends on is **451-blocked from this machine** while `fstream.binance.com` and `data-stream.binance.vision` answer 101 | Measure **how many trigger observations the new contemporaneity marker would exclude** - a construction count on the free per-event record, and therefore executable here - so that the exclusion's bite is known before the entry's outcome is ever measured; and re-state the entry's falsification test against the bounded statistic with the marker attached |

### New this cycle
- **No hypothesis was added and no score moved.** The cycle's output is an **independent re-derivation of CRYPTO-EDGE-003's specification from its own text** on a fresh pull of both legs, which **reproduces the entry's arithmetic to the digit** on everything the text fixes and **fails on everything the text leaves open**; a **window end, an independent-window rule and four slow-clock conventions** fixed in the plan of record; the **minimum detectable effect** written in as a closed form with the block structure declared; a **net-of-carry** requirement for the registered statistic; **CRYPTO-EDGE-001's falsifiability score decided at 4/5** and its **reverse-causality concern discharged** with a pre-registered contemporaneity marker built on the venue's own forced-liquidation record; the **prospective free stream route re-tested** (451 on the host on file, 101 on two alternatives); and **CRYPTO-EDGE-004's own source read as far as access allows**, with its complete abstract and twelve-item reference list obtained free and dated from Crossref's deposited metadata and its free-data score decided at 4/5 with a corrected reason.
- **Four of the catalogue's own recorded numbers are superseded in place, and one of them is impossible.** CRYPTO-EDGE-003's "5,201 matched settlements per instrument (4,037 inside the window)" is replaced by the measured structure (**6,297** total, **1,096** pre-window, **4,037** in-window to 2025-09-08, **5,201** in-window if the window is extended); its **761** pre-window matched settlements are replaced by **1,096**; its alternative reading's **779 / 775**, **333 / 325** and "**192 and 62** independent 20-day windows" are replaced by **903 / 901**, **385 / 375** and **225 / 223** and **74 / 71**; and its slow-clock **57 / 65 extreme weeks and 36 / 39 windows** are replaced by the measured **191 weekly samples, 60 and 64 extreme weeks and 36 windows for both instruments** under the declared conventions.
- **One arithmetic defect in the record is repaired in place.** CRYPTO-EDGE-004's v1.11 clause (ii) printed the exit decomposition with the **entries** counts in the upward-migration slot; the corrected triples are **5,292 exits / 5,098 upward / 194 panel deaths** at the $61m ceiling and **5,337 exits / 5,143 upward / 194 panel deaths with 5,783 entries** at the $60.3m ceiling.
- **The catalogue's first claim that a specification is executable is withdrawn.** The forty-ninth cycle wrote CRYPTO-EDGE-003's order of operations "so that a third party can reproduce the recorded counts from the specification alone"; this cycle's independent implementation shows that the recorded counts are reproducible **only** on the parts the text fixes, that the text's own independent-window rule gives 50% and 17% more windows than the recorded binding numbers, and that the recorded slow-clock pair is not reproducible under any of 96 convention combinations. The specification is now executable, and the recorded numbers it produces have been re-derived and quoted with the conventions attached.




## Previous cycle (forty-ninth research cycle)

A **measurement-and-specification cycle with a claim-side close.** The cycle's five priorities were executed: every total re-derived and every item 16 audited in all three places; a **complete construction census** of CRYPTO-EDGE-001's 281-day linear window; CRYPTO-EDGE-003's **order-of-operations specification** and a **closed trigger-count bracket**; CRYPTO-EDGE-004's **stratum-churn census** and a **fixed boundary-cross-check sample rule**; and the fifth priority in full - the **adversarial re-test of the three carried disagreements** plus the **second-venue forced-liquidation question**, which is now closed with a dated negative and a bounded positive, and a **dated claim-side search** across the three registered predictions. All four totals are **unchanged** (001 = 58, 002 = 46 rejected, 003 = 55, 004 = 48) and **no hypothesis was added**. The cycle's real work was to find out how much of the catalogue's own recorded arithmetic was a property of the *specification* rather than of the data.

**Key findings:**

1. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the construction census is complete and it falsifies one of the catalogue's own claims.** A full pull of the linear archive (**129,640 objects; 64,820 daily zips; 311 symbols; 281 dates**) with the matching metrics file for every symbol-day gives a census of **every contract on every day** - 63,863 perpetual and 957 dated-delivery symbol-days. The catalogue's recorded figures re-derive **to the digit**: **8,218,185 unique forced-liquidation events**, **5,091,109 SELL (61.95%)** and **3,127,076 BUY (38.05%)**; the exact-two row duplication holds in **all 64,820 files** rather than in the eleven the catalogue had sampled; the registered statistic's pooled contract-day median **0.2185%** and p99 **4.2029%**; the daily cross-sectional medians' range **0.0044% to 2.9873%**; the days at or above 0.05% / 0.5% / 1% at **231 / 53 / 16**; and the linear arm's proxy-fidelity band at **p10 2.824% / median 11.668% / p90 57.411%** on **141** positive-reduction days. **But the v1.12 clause's sub-claim that the registered statistic does not carry the small-denominator defect is falsified**: the statistic's maximum is **35,442%** on a contract-day with **$10** of opening open interest value, and its tail is monotone in its denominator. The defect is **relocated**, not removed.

2. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the proxy-fidelity diagnostic is removed from the plan of record, and the cascade day is measured on the arm that contains it.** The diagnostic's value is **monotone in its own denominator** - median **133.1%** when the cross-sectional open-interest reduction is below 5% of its median, **113.0%** at 5-10%, **26.7%** at 10-25%, **12.1%** at 25-100%, **9.7%** at 100% and above - so it cannot separate "the proxy missed the forced flow" from "the reduction was small" and is not a fidelity measure. The forty-eighth cycle's failed transport verdict stands and is not re-run, and the entry is reported on the inverse arm alone. The 2024-08-05 cascade is **outside the linear window** (which ends 2024-03-31), so it was measured on the inverse family: **54 of 54** span-live symbols carry a file, the day carries **6,875 unique events (6,106 SELL)**, and the cross-sectional forced SELL over opening open interest is **2.3183%** against **0.5006%** the day before and a **0.1134%** neighbourhood median - a **4.63x** rise on the day, **20.5x** the neighbourhood median, falling to 0.0965% the next day.

3. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the trigger-count bracket is closed, and closing it supersedes three of the catalogue's own recorded numbers.** The order of operations is now specified end to end: two legs (the publisher's monthly per-symbol funding-rate archive and the second venue's all-swap plus legacy per-instrument daily archives), the 8-hour grid, the legacy leg's settlement offset of one funding interval, the pair difference, a **270-observation trailing band excluding the observation under test**, linear-interpolated percentiles, **the series truncated at the window start**, the 24-hour episode merge, non-overlapping independent windows, the slow clock's **12 weekly observations excluding the current observation**, and a file-level vintage rule. Under it: **738 trigger settlements for BTC (348 upper, 390 lower) and 734 for ETH (338 upper, 396 lower)** out of **3,767 eligible** settlements (19.59% and 19.49%), with **310 and 303 merged episodes** and **180 / 58** and **180 / 56** independent windows at 5 and 20 days. The recorded **741 / 733**, the recorded **18.36% / 18.16%** and the recorded **301** ETH episodes are **superseded in place**, because the recorded percentages were stated against the window's 4,037 settlements of which 270 are structurally ineligible. The forty-eighth cycle's bracket (740 / 739 / 738 and 734 / 734 / 731 / 732 over conventions of 268 to 273) is **confirmed as the same family**.

4. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the slow clock's 36 reproduces for BTC and does not for ETH, and the legs can be carried back before the window.** Under the fixed reading the slow clock gives **36** independent 20-day windows for BTC (57 extreme weeks of 192) and **39** for ETH (65 extreme weeks); the forty-eighth cycle's statement that ETH reproduces 36 on a 13-observation band including it is **not reproducible**, and across 32 conventions the count runs **24-40** for BTC and **26-41** for ETH. A **new fact the record did not have**: both legs reach back to **2020-12-31 16:00**, giving **761 pre-window matched settlements** and a warm trailing band at the window's start; under that reading the counts are **779 / 775**, **333 / 325** episodes and **192 / 62** independent 20-day windows, so the choice of truncation is worth **41 trigger settlements, 23 episodes and 4 independent windows** for BTC. The publisher leg's **114** monthly files are recorded vintage by vintage: minimum lag **28 days**, maximum **493 days**, **30 files backfilled in one batch on 2023-05-09**, 57 distinct publication dates, 76 distinct published timestamps, no value changed between vintages.

5. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 - the churn census is executed, the catalogue is mixing two ceilings, and one of its own recorded figures does not reproduce.** On the 2,850-date registered panel window (2019-01-01 to 2026-10-20) the panel structure reproduces exactly (**850** assets with the estimated metric, **135** with the current metric, **84** with both). At the plan of record's **$60.3m** ceiling the final-date stratum is **447 / 32** and the size series re-derives as 60, 100, 137, 46, 206, 144, 147, 388, 488, 447; at the **$61m** ceiling the catalogue's recorded ten-date size series reproduces **to the digit** and so does the recorded exit decomposition (**5,292 exits, 5,098 upward, 194 panel deaths**). The thirty-third cycle's **5,323 / 5,286 / 225** figures **do not reproduce under either ceiling** and are superseded in place. **The two metrics' churn differs in membership but not in rate**: on the 84-asset common panel the current metric's stratum holds **100.00%** median 30-day retention against the estimated metric's **95.24%**, but the daily turnover rate is **1.18%** against **1.14%** of the median stratum. Stablecoin membership (a stated 20-ticker pegged list) and deprecated-token membership (the catalogue's 21 named assets) are declared per date.

6. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 - the boundary cross-check's sample rule is fixed, and the recorded pair was a matching artefact.** The rule is: the venue's keyless top-**500** screen fetched as five pages of `per_page=100`; the **normalised full name** as the only stable identifier, with **no symbol matching at all**; collisions excluded; a **single fixed date** (2026-10-20); the ratio as panel capitalisation over reference capitalisation with the **median** as the summary statistic; and the **matched-name set reported alongside the ratio**. Under it the re-derived pair is **estimated 0.9796 (n = 254, 100% within [0.5, 2]) and current 0.9931 (n = 38, 92.1% within [0.5, 2])**, with the current metric's only outliers lpt 0.490, xlm 2.906 and gno 3.812. The forty-eighth cycle's **0.9936 (n = 277)** and **1.0021 (n = 44)** do **not** reproduce, and its six estimated-metric outliers do not either - under exact normalised-name matching the estimated metric has **no outlier at all**, so the disagreement between the two definitions' cross-checks is **largely a matching artefact, not a property of the two metrics**.

8. **The second-venue forced-liquidation question is closed, and the answer is a dated negative with a bounded positive.** The forty-eighth cycle's fifth priority asked whether a second centralised venue publishes or retains a free, dated, per-contract, per-event forced-liquidation record deep enough to carry CRYPTO-EDGE-001's pre-registered T2 test (Spearman rho >= 0.50, disagreement rate <= 30% on opposite sides of the pooled daily median, positive slope, 60 matched days minimum). **No centralised venue publishes one that is free and deep enough to carry it.** The second venue used for CRYPTO-EDGE-003's funding leg has **no free per-event liquidation archive at all** - its free monthly archive carries trades and swap-rate files only, with no liquidation file type, and its live `liquidation-orders` route retains about 24 hours to three days depending on the read. Bybit's public archive has **no liquidations directory** and its liquidations are WebSocket-only; Bitget's per-event route is documented at **three days**; Kraken's futures-history API exposes **no liquidation route** and its executions endpoint ignores `from`/`to`; Coinbase and MEXC expose no public liquidation history; HTX has the best-documented per-event schema of the majors but its retention was not tested. **The deepest free per-event record located anywhere is Gate.io's `GET /api/v4/futures/{settle}/liq_orders`**, whose empirical retention bracket lies **between 2025-10-30 and 2025-11-01** - about **11.7 months** at the access date - and whose query window is capped at 3,600 seconds, so it is paged rather than ranged. That depth is **three weeks short of the 2025-10-10 cascade** and far short of a multi-year sample, so **T2 is unexecutable on free data** and any multi-year reconstruction of the 2022-2025 crash record requires a paid archive (Tardis.dev, CoinGlass or Amberdata/Kaiko). **No score moved** - it is an access fact about a measuring instrument, and it *strengthens* the ground on which CRYPTO-EDGE-001's falsifiability is held at 4/5. It is written into the plan of record at CRYPTO-EDGE-001 item 14, v1.13 (vi).

9. **The claim-side search is executed, and its principal result is a source the catalogue has cited for seventeen cycles and has never been able to read.** **SSRN 6648082 (Zhang and Makgolo), "Cross-Sectional Dispersion and the State Dependence of Cryptocurrency Momentum"** is CRYPTO-EDGE-004's own published source, posted 2026-04-25 and revised 2026-04-27, and **SSRN returns "Content Blocked" (403) to every fetch**, so the catalogue has carried it for seventeen cycles on the strength of its abstract and its search-index snippets. The snippets report **lagged cross-sectional dispersion** as the variable most associated with a subsequent momentum breakdown, an effect that survives **controls for BTC realised volatility and average cross-asset correlation**, is **nonlinear and concentrated in the upper tail** of dispersion, and improves drawdown and constant-exposure outcomes under dispersion-based scaling. **The construct is not the entry's construct** - the state variable is the dispersion of *returns* across assets, it is *lagged*, and the conditioning is *orthogonalised* - so **no score moves**, the entry's registered claim remains untested, and the full text is recorded as a deferral with a named missing input: it is the highest-value next read for CRYPTO-EDGE-004. Two non-crypto precedents read in snippet form **disagree in sign** (equities: dispersion *negatively* related to the subsequent momentum premium; FX: dispersion *amplifying* momentum), which is a specification constraint rather than a result. For CRYPTO-EDGE-001, the full-text-read seven-cascade study (arXiv:2607.27070) finds the only regularity common to all seven events to be a **compression of taker order-flow variance**, with price critical-slowing-down present in only **5 of 7** events - it **does not test basis or funding extremes**, so it neither supports nor refutes the entry's predictor, but it establishes that early-warning signals are **event-heterogeneous**; a named **reverse-causality** concern was also found (liquidation-driven crashes generate large **negative basis spikes**, SSRN 6185958), so basis may be a symptom rather than a cause and the entry's forward registration must exclude that reading operationally. For CRYPTO-EDGE-003 the claim-side evidence runs against the entry's own prior and is recorded as such: a two-month, 26-exchange study reports **GARCH persistence of BTC funding spreads near unity**, while a separate 26-venue study reports cross-venue funding spreads **mean-reverting over an eight-day window**; neither speaks to the slow clock's resolution or to venue-relative forward returns, so no dimension moves. The public research ledger was re-read and re-verified: **H08** (funding extremes, -0.95% at 7 days over 17,910 symbol-days) and **H10** (spot-vs-perp basis, -2.61%, CI [-4.88%, -0.12%], over 17,899 symbol-days) **reproduce the catalogue's recorded point estimates to the digit**; **no hypothesis file exists after 2026-08-28**; and **no ledger entry duplicates any of the three live entries' registered claims**.

7. **Audit and adversarial.** The three item-14 clauses the forty-eighth cycle wrote are internally consistent and their arithmetic reconciles to the digit. The three alternative placements the forty-eighth cycle named were re-derived and check out: 003 free-data 5/5 -> 4/5 = 55 -> **52**; 004 robustness 1/5 -> 2/5 = 48 -> **51**; 004 free-data 4/5 -> 5/5 = 48 -> **51**. Item 16 was audited in all three places for all four entries, with the **tense** of every historical narrative checked, and **no stale score remains**. All three carried disagreements were re-tested and upheld (001 falsifiability **4/5**, 003 free-data **5/5**, 004 free-data **4/5**); one **new** placement is recorded rather than dismissed (001 falsifiability 4/5 -> 3/5, total 56, on the ground that the registered statistic is an unbounded ratio in its pooled form and the transport instrument has been removed) and is declined because the statistic is specified on a bounded region - the daily cross-sectional median, 0.0044% to 2.9873%.

### New this cycle
- **No hypothesis was added and no score moved.** The cycle's output is a **complete construction census** of CRYPTO-EDGE-001's linear window (every contract, every day, with the metrics file for each symbol-day), the **removal of the proxy-fidelity instrument from the plan of record** on a measured confound, the **order-of-operations specification and closed trigger-count bracket** for CRYPTO-EDGE-003 with the **eligible denominator** stated, the **stratum-churn census** for CRYPTO-EDGE-004 with the **two-ceiling defect** corrected in place, the **fixed boundary-cross-check sample rule** and its re-derived pair, the file-by-file **vintage table** for the publisher's funding leg, and the adversarial re-test of all three carried disagreements with one new placement recorded and declined, the **second-venue forced-liquidation question closed with a dated negative and a bounded positive** (T2 unexecutable on free data; the deepest free per-event record is Gate.io at about 11.7 months, three weeks short of the 2025-10-10 cascade), and a **dated claim-side search** whose principal result is that CRYPTO-EDGE-004's own published source, SSRN 6648082, has been carried unread for seventeen cycles and is now the highest-value next read.

- **One of the catalogue's own claims is falsified and withdrawn in place.** The v1.12 clause's sub-claim that the registered statistic does not carry the small-denominator defect is false: the defect is relocated to the level of the opening open interest, not removed, and the statistic's maximum over the window is **35,442%**.

## Previous cycle (forty-eighth research cycle)

A **confirmation-execution cycle.** The forty-seventh cycle left three confirmations in the plan of record - CRYPTO-EDGE-001's family-transport test (T1), CRYPTO-EDGE-003's funding-leg vintage question and CRYPTO-EDGE-004's within-asset market-cap metric comparison - and this cycle **executed all three**, with each scope fixed in advance and written down before any data file was read. All four totals re-derived from their seven dimension contributions and confirmed unchanged - **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 = 58** (16+6+3+15+6+8+4), **CRYPTO-EDGE-002 = 46** (rejected; 12+3+3+15+3+6+4), **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 = 55** (12+3+6+15+3+8+8), **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 = 48** (12+6+3+12+3+8+4). **Two of the three confirmations resolve against the catalogue's own record.**

**Key findings:**

1. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the family-transport test is executed and FAILS.** On a complete pull of **12,144** archive files covering both arms, both products and every day of both windows, with the cross-section fixed at the **eight family-analogue perpetual pairs** and all dated delivery contracts excluded, the inverse (COIN-M) arm's forced-SELL-to-open-interest-reduction ratio falls **outside** the linear (USDⓈ-M) arm's pre-declared band on **13 of 54** matched days. The linear arm's band re-derives to p10 **2.824%** / median **11.668%** / p90 **57.411%** (the recorded p90 **57.41%** reproduces exactly), and the verdict is robust - identical breach set under the re-derived band, **8** breaches on 45 days at 8-of-8 usable pairs, **15** breaches on 59 days at 4-or-more. Per the pre-registered rule, **the substitution is retired and the entry must be reported on the inverse arm alone**. The two families' statistics differ systematically, not in the tail: matched-day medians **10.350% inverse against 15.731% linear**, the inverse arm below the linear arm on **32 of 54** days, and **14 of 54** breaches against the linear arm's *matched-day* band.

2. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the transport criterion was defective, and the defect is recorded rather than used.** The band is estimated on a superset of the matched days and then applied pointwise to a subset, and **the linear arm itself breaches its own band on 11 of the same 54 matched days**. The failed verdict is reported as it stands; the criterion is repaired for future use only (the band must be estimated on the matched days themselves), with no re-run and no re-specification of the result.

3. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the open figure from the last cycle is resolved, and the resolution is a two-population defect.** The recorded "41.24% (2024-08-07, **8 contracts**)" is **exactly** the SELL-side ratio over the eight family-analogue contracts, while 2024-08-04 (**24.08%**), 2024-08-05 (**11.20%**) and 2024-08-06 (**3.37%**) are the SELL-side ratios over **all 35, 36 and 31** perpetuals with a file. On a consistent all-perpetual construction 2024-08-07 is **31.11%** SELL-side and **41.09%** including the forced buys. Two recorded linear-arm figures are also corrected in place: the recorded per-contract **maximum of 25.02%** is below its own recorded p90 of **47.90%** and is impossible (the re-derived maximum is **2,502.03%**, with **198** contract-days above 25.02%), and the recorded positive-reduction day count (**142**) does not reproduce (**141**).

4. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the vintage question is closed: an earlier vintage IS obtainable, and the backfill changed nothing.** The venue's **legacy per-instrument daily product** was pulled in full for both instruments over 2022-01-01 to 2025-09-07 (**2,692** files); **none** was published on or after 2025-12-01, its maximum publication date is **2025-09-07**, and it carries the same settlements as the re-published all-swap product. The cross-vintage comparison over **3,989 settlements per instrument finds 0 differing values**, so the rolling backfill re-published 1,356 files without altering a single funding rate. The other routes return nothing: no versioned path, no checksum manifest, `www.okx.com` serving identical bytes, `static.okex.com` and `cdn.okex.com` not resolving, no Internet Archive capture of the venue's tree.

5. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the recorded counts re-derive exactly, on both vintages.** **5,201** matched settlements per instrument, **4,037** inside the window, **310** and **301** merged episodes, **180** independent 5-day windows, **58** instantaneous 20-day windows (BTC) and the entry's binding **36** slow-clock 20-day windows all reproduce; the trigger counts are bracketed at **738-740** (BTC) and **731-734** (ETH) against the recorded 741 and 733, a ≤3-settlement effect of an undocumented trailing-window convention. The publisher's leg is versioned too (all **114** monthly files lag at least 28 days, **30** backfilled in one batch on 2023-05-09) and has no obtainable earlier copy.

6. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 - the within-asset metric comparison is executed, and the composition confound is decomposed.** On the **84**-asset common panel (**83** with a common window; **197,747** asset-days), the estimated-over-current ratio has median **0.9489**, and the two definitions place an asset in **different strata on 9,428 of 197,747 asset-days (4.77%)**, affecting **44 of the 83 assets** at some point. The panel-wide composition figure reproduces exactly (**447** / **32** / **22**, Jaccard **0.0481**) and is decomposed: on the disjoint remainder the two definitions' sub-ceiling strata are **completely disjoint** (**423** against **9**, intersection **0**, Jaccard **0.000**), so the whole composition gap is generated by the 84 assets carrying both metrics.

7. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 - the boundary cross-check is re-derived per metric and the recorded pair does not reproduce.** Estimated **median 0.9936** (n = 277, **97.8%** within [0.5, 2]); current **median 1.0021** (n = 44, **93.2%** within [0.5, 2]), against the recorded **0.97 / 0.98**. The **outlier set reproduces exactly** (`u`, `pros`, `lit`, `cusd`, `gusd`, `data`; `gusd`, `xlm`, `gno`) and the recorded current-metric IQR lower bound of 0.49 is not reproducible from today's 44 matched names. The recorded pair is superseded in place by the re-derived pair, and the check must fix a sample rule before it is quoted again.

8. **Adversarial.** All three carried disagreements re-tested on their own terms and upheld (001 falsifiability 4/5; 003 free-data 5/5; 004 free-data 4/5). The counter-arguments this cycle's work might have moved a dimension - 001 robustness 1/5 -> 2/5 and 001 free-data 5/5 -> 4/5 on the transport failure, 003 free-data 5/5 -> 4/5 on the versioned leg, 004 robustness 1/5 -> 2/5 on the metric-determined stratum - are each declined with the reason recorded in the entry's item 16, and the alternative placements are recorded rather than dismissed.

- **No hypothesis was added and no score moved.** The cycle's output is the **execution of three pre-registered confirmations**, two of which resolve against the catalogue's own record, and two **corrections in place** to recorded figures that could not be reproduced (the CRYPTO-EDGE-001 per-contract maximum of 25.02%, which sits below its own recorded p90 of 47.90%, and the CRYPTO-EDGE-004 boundary-cross-check pair of 0.97 / 0.98). The 003 clause now carries the vintage rule's completed form (3,989 settlements compared, 0 differing), the trigger-count bracket (738-740 / 731-734) and the requirement to fix the slow clock's trailing band **in observations**; the 004 clause now carries the executed within-asset result (4.77% of asset-days change stratum, 44 of 83 assets affected), the superseded cross-check pair (**0.9936 / 1.0021** in place of 0.97 / 0.98), a required **sample rule** for that check, and a new binding clause for the stratum's stability.

## Previous cycle (forty-seventh research cycle)

A **specification-completeness audit, dated coverage census and adversarial re-test cycle.** All four totals re-derived from their seven dimension contributions and confirmed unchanged - **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 = 58** (16+6+3+15+6+8+4), **CRYPTO-EDGE-002 = 46** (rejected; 12+3+3+15+3+6+4), **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 = 55** (12+3+6+15+3+8+8), **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 = 48** (12+6+3+12+3+8+4); rank order unchanged (001 > 003 > 004 by 3 and 7 points); all confidence low. Item 16 audited in all three places (contribution list, versioned narratives, unversioned rationale prose) with no stale scores remaining: 001 clean; 002 clean; 003's contribution list corrected to the seventeenth-cycle decomposition (12+3+6+15+3+8+8) in the twenty-third cycle; 004's rationale prose version-stamped as v1.1 in the thirty-second cycle with current state stated alongside. One duplicated fragment in CRYPTO-EDGE-003's item 1 version history was found and corrected in place. No hypothesis added; no score moved; no empirical validation performed; no return, spread, drawdown, hit rate, win rate, Sharpe ratio or strategy-performance quantity computed for any strategy.

**Key findings:**

1. **The principal finding: none of the three priority confirmations was in the record it governs.** The transport pre-registration (001), the effective-sample and minimum-detectable-effect clauses (003) and the market-cap metric pre-registration (004) existed only in appended amendment paragraphs after item 16, in this shortlist, in the changelog and in the handoffs - **not in the item-14 validation plan of the entry each governs**, which is the field a validator executes. The thirty-sixth cycle required exactly this for 003 ("the entry's item 14 must carry this as a binding clause rather than an advisory note") and did not do it. **All three are written into their entries' item 14 in this cycle**, version-stamped v1.11, v1.9 and v1.9. From this cycle a confirmation is a **write**, not a re-statement, and the write's location is recorded.

2. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the transport pre-registration is completed with the numbers it named, and the two transports it conflated are separated.** The pre-registration named a pre-declared rank-correlation floor, a pre-declared disagreement ceiling and a pre-declared disagreement threshold and **declared none of them** - an exhaustive scan of the five artefacts and both handoffs finds no numeric floor, ceiling or threshold anywhere in the catalogue. The cross-venue data does not exist (the second venue's per-contract per-event route retains about 24 hours), so the numbers are fixed now, before any data exists: **Spearman rho >= 0.50**; a **disagreement rate defined as the two venues' values falling on opposite sides of the pooled daily median, capped at 30%** (independence expects 50%); a **positive slope** requirement with the magnitude reported but not tested; and a **60-matched-day minimum** before the test is read. The record conflated **T1, the family transport** (linear against inverse on the same venue, executable now, criterion = the linear arm's p10-p90 band of 2.73% and 57.41%) with **T2, the venue transport** (prospective only, and not producible from a single-venue arm); the two are now named separately, each with its own criterion, availability status and consequence. T1 is made executable by carrying over the band's conditioning (positive cross-sectional reduction on both arms), fixing the aggregation (sum of contracts) and defining matched days.

3. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 - the arm's length was wrong, and a dated census of both arms adds two facts.** The inverse arm is a **478-calendar-day** window (2023-06-25 to 2024-10-14) extending **197 days** beyond the linear arm, not the "199-day inverse arm" the shortlist, changelog and both handoffs carry; corrected in place wherever it appears. The inverse panel has **twelve** near-empty dates (at least 80% of live symbols absent) where the catalogue records one, and the **linear arm has none** - the arms differ in coverage shape as well as rate (2.30% against 35.97% absence). The cascade day 2024-08-05 is **fully covered** (54 of 54 span-live symbols), of which **36 are perpetuals** - the recorded live universe, all with a file - and **18 are quarterly delivery contracts** that must be excluded rather than pooled. The publisher's product has **not** been extended: the relocated USD-M product still ends **2024-03-31** across 311 symbol directories and the inverse family **2024-10-14** across 118, so the entry's critical regime remains unreachable on any free native route.

4. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 - the funding leg is versioned data, and the entry carried no vintage rule.** The venue's all-swap daily archive re-verifies live (September 2021 island present, main interval 2022-01-17 to 2026-10-20, 1,738 files, 2026-10-21 not yet published), but every sampled file for 2022-01-17 to 2025-09-04 carries a `Last-Modified` **later** than its own date, and the re-publication ran backwards day by day from 2025-12-14 to a completion on **2026-02-02**, covering the entry's entire recorded window. The recorded counts (5,201 matched settlements per instrument, 741 trigger settlements, 310 episodes, 180/58/36 windows) are therefore counts **on the post-backfill vintage**, and a validator holding a pre-December-2025 copy holds different bytes for the same dates. A **vintage rule** is now binding in item 14, in the same form as the sibling entry's Binance-archive versioning rule. Item 14 now also carries, as binding clauses, the independent-window count at each registered horizon, the block structure, the minimum detectable effect, and the plain answer that **the registered null is not publishable** as evidence of no effect without a pre-registered MDE.

5. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 - the metric-sensitivity question becomes separable for the first time.** The community tier's catalogue re-verifies today and the recorded counts reproduce to the digit (6,347 assets; `CapMrktCurUSD` on **135**; `CapMrktEstUSD` on **850**; **901** distinct), and the recorded parameter requirement reproduces (the timeseries route returns HTTP 400 without an explicit `assets` parameter). **New: 84 assets carry both metrics, and on only 19 of them do the two metrics start on the same date** - the estimated metric starts 2019-06-22 on 133 of its 850 assets while the current metric's starts spread over 2010 to 2025 - so an **84-asset common panel** exists on which the two definitions can be compared for the same asset and date, free of the composition confound, provided the comparison runs on a **per-asset common window**. Item 14 now carries the decomposition (within-asset on the common panel, population-composition across the disjoint remainder), the **per-metric** boundary cross-check (0.97 estimated, 0.98 current) and the substituted screens' size-composition declaration as binding clauses.

6. **Adversarial.** All three carried disagreements re-tested on their own terms and upheld (001 falsifiability 4/5; 003 free-data 5/5; 004 free-data 4/5). The counter-arguments that this cycle's work might have moved a dimension (001 falsifiability to 5/5, 003 falsifiability to 5/5, 004 robustness to 2/5) are recorded in each entry's item 16 with the reason they are declined, so that they can be audited and disagreed with. Guarded against re-spins disguised as data improvements: a data improvement requires a specification change with an explicit falsifier, and none was accepted. No new hypothesis added; none of substantive value.
