Ninth-cycle totals: 001 = 16+6+3+15+6+8+4 = 58; 002 = 12+3+3+15+3+6+4 = 46 (rejected); 003 = 12+6+6+12+3+8+8 = 55; 004 = 12+6+3+9+3+8+4 = 45. The margin between rank 1 and rank 2 is 3 points, down from 6 - a sign that the top of the ranking is unsettled, not that either entry improved.

### New this cycle
- **No hypothesis was added, and that is a deliberate choice with a stated reason.** The strongest candidate that the new data capability enables - a contract-life-cycle effect in which the perp's premium over spot and its funding rate decay over the first weeks after listing, because arbitrage capital has not yet deployed - has a coherent mechanism (the funding clamp and the capital-dependent stability band of He, Wang and Zhang 2026) but no direct published test, and a plausible magnitude below the round-trip cost of the instruments it would trade, which is the same net-value objection that already killed two entries in this catalogue. It is not proposed. No new derivative hypothesis was added either: the domain's published and preregistered evidence remains crowded, and nothing this cycle left a clean opening.
- **One family was searched, found and rejected on documented cost grounds** and recorded in REJECTED_AND_DEFERRED.md: flow-conditioned short-horizon reversal as a retail-scale crypto return source. Reinstatement condition: a round-trip cost below roughly 1.3 bp.
- **One family was searched, found and deferred on an identification ground** and recorded in REJECTED_AND_DEFERRED.md: venue-announced perpetual delisting as a cross-sectional event. It is feasible on free data for the first time, but the announcement is a deterministic function of the token's weakness, so any measured effect is confounded with information drift unless a matched control of weak non-delisted contracts is preregistered.

### Downgraded / changed this cycle
- **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 downgraded 61 -> 58**, confidence low. Evidence quality 3/5 -> 2/5 only. Mechanism, robustness, free-data accessibility, net value, falsifiability and originality held.
- **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 unchanged at 55**, confidence low. No new direct evidence in either direction; two adjacent results recorded without moving a score.
- **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 reinstated at 45** (deferred -> active, rank 3). Free-data accessibility 2/5 -> 3/5 and net value after frictions 2/5 -> 1/5; total unchanged, status and risk profile not.

## Previous cycle (eighth research cycle)

Scoring audit confirmed: all four catalogue totals re-derived from the seven dimension contributions (score / 5 x weight) and checked against IDEA_LEDGER.md; every total is correct — CRYPTO-EDGE-001 = 61 (16+9+3+15+6+8+4), CRYPTO-EDGE-002 = 46 (rejected), CRYPTO-EDGE-003 = 55 (12+6+6+12+3+8+8), CRYPTO-EDGE-004 = 45 (12+6+3+6+6+8+4, deferred) — and the rank order is unchanged. No hypothesis added, re-scored, promoted or demoted.

- **Binary data question resolved (determination, not opinion):** NO free, dated, point-in-time source of historical circulating supply, historical market capitalisation, market-cap rank, or a dated delisting record for tokens delisted by a major venue was confirmed. Checked directly: CoinGecko free tier rejects historical queries (HTTP 401, error_code 10012, 365-day limit); CoinLore is current-only (chart endpoint 404); DefiLlama free tier gives current per-token supply and aggregate stablecoin history but per-token historical supply and vesting schedules require a Pro key; CoinMarketCap keyless tier is current-only with historical listings paywalled; the Binance public archive supplies delisted-symbol prices but no dated delisting record. CRYPTO-EDGE-004 and the token-dilution family therefore remain deferred on a data-access ground; the catalogue has no active non-derivatives member.

- **Third non-derivatives family:** searched; no candidate cleared the free-data bar (staking/liquid-staking basis, token burns, credit spreads, on-chain reserves, stablecoin flows surveyed — each lacks free point-in-time inputs or is already covered/deferred/rejected). No new hypothesis added; none of substantive value.

- **Open questions carried forward:** CRYPTO-EDGE-001 crash conditionality on retail perps post-July-2024 (untested externally); CRYPTO-EDGE-003 sign on a slow clock (two-sided, contested); conditional funding-extreme split (Shek 2026 deferred, independent replication open); single-source risk partially discharged (CoinGecko limit confirmed by direct fetch).

No empirical strategy validation was performed in this cycle; no strategy executed; no return, Sharpe or drawdown figure computed.

The eighth cycle's "no dated delisting record on free data" finding was **wrong**, and the ninth cycle records why: the Binance public archive *does* carry a dated delisting record for venue-delisted contracts, encoded not as a field but as the **absence of trades** in the terminal daily kline file of a contract that has ceased trading. CRYPTO-EDGE-004 was therefore reinstated. The eighth cycle's other determinations stand: historical circulating supply, historical market capitalisation and market-cap rank remain unconfirmed on any free tier, and the token-dilution family remains deferred on that ground. See the ninth-cycle section above and PROCESS_CHANGELOG.md.)*

## Previous cycle (seventh research cycle)

No hypothesis added, one deferred, three re-scored, and the catalogue's own arithmetic corrected.

1. **The catalogue's totals were wrong, and the error was in the rank-1 entry.** CRYPTO-EDGE-001's total was recorded as 63 while its own dimension contributions summed to 67 (16+12+6+15+6+8+4), and its pre-cycle-6 total of 73 less the two documented v1.2 downgrades is also 67. All four totals were re-derived from the dimension contributions in this cycle; the other three entries were already correct. The error did not change the rank order, but it had been propagated into the shortlist, the ranking table and the handoff, and it understated the gap to second place by four points. The process change that follows: totals are re-derived from the dimension contributions every cycle, and the shortlist table is checked against the ledger before publication.

2. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 is downgraded to 61/100, confidence low.** Two new findings bite, and they point in opposite directions. The published carry-crash paper's *own* crash-risk section is mined for the first time in this catalogue, and it directly supports the tail form of the prediction on dated futures: carry is strongly negatively related to implied and to realized skewness, a rise in carry of 10% predicts a 44% increase in total sell liquidations relative to total open interest over the next month, and carry predicts sell but not long liquidations. More damagingly, the *same authors'* earlier working paper measures carry as the funding rate of BTC/USD perpetual swaps and reports that it *positively* predicts returns in the BTC time series and in a cross-section of 51 cryptocurrencies. The published crash coefficient is a dated-futures coefficient; the entry's target instrument is the perpetual, and on the perpetual the original authors' own earlier result has the opposite mean sign. Added to that: four independent perpetual-era tests of funding and basis extremes find no unconditional edge and document cross-asset sign heterogeneity (BTC negative, ETH/BNB/XRP/DOGE positive, on a 789-day, three-venue, six-asset sample); the registered absolute trigger is unattainable on perps in the current regime (30-day basis about 5.2% annualized against a 4.5% T-bill rate, and the three-month basis below the two-year Treasury for 157 consecutive days since February 2026); and the 2025 cascade evidence runs the mechanism in the *opposite* direction, through basis compression and carry-trade unwinding rather than an elevated basis level. Evidence quality 4/5 -> 3/5, robustness 2/5 -> 1/5.

3. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 is downgraded to 55/100, and the change is to the construction rather than to the claim.** The dispersion *level* has a documented half-life of 1.5 to 3.5 hours in a two-year, four-venue study, and a separate 26-exchange microstructure study finds intraday spread autocorrelations above 0.96 (ETH reverting in about 20 minutes) with spreads peaking about two hours after each settlement; a trailing-decile threshold on a quantity that reverts within hours is close to trading noise, so the signal must be re-registered on a slow clock. Against that, the *price-discovery objection* that dominated the last cycle's downgrade is now documented to be a poor general rule: the same 2026 study finds Bybit Granger-causing Binance funding rates in 62% of rolling windows against 21% in the reverse direction, and OKX 55% against 25%, so information discovery in funding rates does not follow a size hierarchy. Net value after frictions 2/5 -> 1/5, because the only direct cost test of the entry's registered construction finds that a threshold rule earns the largest gross income of any rule tested and finishes at -9% to -15% annualized after costs, while the construction that clears costs is a slow patient rebalance that does not use extreme-dispersion thresholds at all.

4. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 is deferred at 45/100 and moves off the active shortlist.** The catalogue's own open question - is a survivorship-aware point-in-time crypto universe constructible from free data? - is now answered, and the answer is partly no. The CoinGecko free tier rejects every historical query (HTTP 401, error_code 10012, "Public API users are limited to querying historical data within the past 365 days"), so no free historical market-cap or market-cap-rank series back to 2019 is available there; CoinLore's free API is current-only. A listing-date and trailing-volume screen *is* constructible on free data from the Binance public archive, and a preregistered audit of exactly that construction retained 58% of Sharpe (1.47 to 0.86) and 49% of CAGR (+42.91% to +21.06%) for an unchanged cross-sectional momentum book, cleared neither a Sharpe of 1.0 nor a deflated-Sharpe bar of 0.95, and reversed its best year. The *delisting* half of survivorship is not observable on free data at all, and high cross-sectional dispersion - the entry's own test regime - is precisely where launches and delistings concentrate. Worse for the claim: the closest available preregistered test of the mechanism, a dispersion dial conditioning the same momentum book on cross-sectional dispersion, was **killed**, with the spread monotone *increasing* across dispersion terciles (+0.50%, +1.05%, +1.40%) and a top-minus-bottom difference of +0.90% with a 95% interval of [-2.66%, +3.69%] - the opposite direction to the entry's registered prediction - and a breadth-dial sibling was killed on the same pattern. Deferred, not rejected: the entry's registered prediction has not been tested and failed, and the falsification-handling rule is therefore not engaged.

5. **A second non-derivatives family was found, and deferred on a data-access ground rather than on evidence.** Token dilution and supply-schedule premia have a strong, recent, published result: 404 coins, weekly, 2020-2026, with the fully diluted valuation premium and a 12-week dilution rate predicting cross-sectional returns at Newey-West t-statistics of -3.13 and -2.79, portfolio sorts yielding 25-32% annualized long-short spreads, the premium absent in blue chips and mature coins and concentrated in the first year of trading, and a mechanism of *limited investor attention to disclosed vesting schedules* rather than mechanical price pressure. It is blocked by the same CoinGecko free-tier history restriction, because the signal needs dated circulating supply, fully diluted valuation and vesting schedules, and no free source of historical circulating supply was confirmed. The reinstatement condition - a free, dated, point-in-time supply or delisting record - is the same condition that would unblock or retire CRYPTO-EDGE-004, so the two families now share one decisive data question.

6. **Standing prior recorded for the next cycle, and it is sharper than last cycle's.** The carry and crowding family in crypto is *instrument-dependent*, not simply "continuation beats exhaustion". On dated futures, high carry predicts crashes (peer-reviewed, sample to July 2024). On perpetual funding, carry positively predicts returns (the same authors, 2021 working paper). On retail perps in 2024-2026, both the unconditional contrarian and the unconditional continuation forms fail, and the sign of any effect depends on the direction of the market on the event day and on the asset. Any new candidate in this domain must specify its instrument, its asset class and its conditioning state before it can be assessed, and **no candidate in this family may be promoted on the dated-futures evidence alone**.

## New this cycle
- **No hypothesis was added.** One new non-derivatives family - token dilution and supply-schedule premia - was identified, assessed, and recorded in REJECTED_AND_DEFERRED.md as a deferral, not a rejection. The published result is strong (404 coins, Newey-West t of -3.13 and -2.79, 25-32% annualized long-short spreads) and the mechanism is clean (limited investor attention to disclosed vesting schedules); the blocker is that the essential input - dated circulating supply, fully diluted valuation and vesting schedules - is not confirmed obtainable on free data, because the CoinGecko free tier refuses historical queries and no alternative free source of historical circulating supply was found. Promoting it would have violated the free-data standard.
- No new derivative hypothesis was added either. The domain's published and preregistered evidence was surveyed and is now *more* crowded, not less: a conditional funding-arbitrage-capacity result, a funding-drag result, an online-learning result, a cross-venue carry robustness result, an ETH-BTC funding-differential result and two microstructure studies were all read, and they do not leave a clean opening.

## Downgraded / deferred this cycle
- **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 downgraded 67 (corrected) -> 61**, confidence low-medium -> low. Evidence quality 4/5 -> 3/5 and robustness 2/5 -> 1/5. Mechanism, free-data accessibility, net value after frictions, falsifiability and originality held. Reason recorded in IDEA_LEDGER.md item 16.
- **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 downgraded 58 -> 55**, confidence low. Net value after frictions 2/5 -> 1/5. Robustness deliberately held at 2/5 because this cycle's new evidence is two-sided: adverse on the construction's half-life, favourable on the price-discovery objection. Mechanism, evidence quality, free-data accessibility, falsifiability and originality held.
- **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 downgraded 56 -> 45 and deferred**, confidence low. Evidence quality 3/5 -> 2/5, robustness 2/5 -> 1/5, free-data accessibility 3/5 -> 2/5, originality 3/5 -> 2/5. Deferred rather than rejected because its registered prediction has not been tested and failed.
- **The catalogue is left with no active non-derivatives member.** That is a stated loss of diversification, not a rounding error, and it is the clearest single argument for the next cycle's work.

## Previous cycle (sixth research cycle)

One hypothesis rejected, two downgraded, one new non-derivatives entry. The cycle's dominant finding is a sign reversal that reorganises the catalogue.

One hypothesis rejected, two downgraded, one new non-derivatives entry. The cycle's dominant finding is a sign reversal that reorganises the catalogue.
1. **A preregistered external test of the funding-extreme contrarian thesis failed with the wrong sign**, so CRYPTO-EDGE-002 is rejected rather than downgraded. The test (Binance USDT perpetuals, 8 symbols, 17,910 symbol-days, December 2019 to July 2026) used this catalogue's own signal construction — trailing-90-day percentile of daily funding, 10th/90th deciles, fixed before the run — and found 7-day LOW-minus-HIGH of -0.95% with a 95% block-bootstrap interval of [-3.20%, +1.39%] and sign consistency of 4 of 8 symbols against a required 5. Both point-estimate directions leaned the wrong way: HIGH-funding days were followed by higher returns (+3.30% vs +2.35% at 7 days; +12.61% vs +4.83% at 30 days). A momentum-flavored variant was also tested and not promoted, so both signs are closed. This closes the catalogue's largest evidence gap for CRYPTO-EDGE-002, which until now rested only on practitioner anecdote.
2. **The same reversal hits CRYPTO-EDGE-001.** A preregistered test of the perp-basis level on Binance perpetuals (17,899 symbol-days) found the 7-day LOW-minus-HIGH at -2.61% with an interval of [-4.88%, -0.12%] excluding zero *on the wrong side*: high-premium days were followed by +4.20% vs +1.59% at 7 days and +14.8% vs +6.1% at 30 days. This is the closest available test of the entry's directional claim, it covers the post-July-2024 window that no published sample covers, and it contradicts the mean-return form of the prediction. CRYPTO-EDGE-001 is therefore restructured from a directional return claim into a **crash-conditionality** claim, with a mandatory volatility-matched baseline, and is downgraded from 73 to 63.
3. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 is downgraded from 61 to 58** because its sign is now contested rather than merely uncertain: three independent sources place price discovery on the more heavily traded and typically higher-funding venue, and a preregistered cross-venue test found the high-premium venue was followed by *higher* returns (+3.17% at 7 days, CI [+0.51%, +6.08%], breadth 17/20, surviving a trailing-return trim and a concentration check). The same test found cross-venue dispersion *as a magnitude* to be noise at all three horizons. CRYPTO-EDGE-003's validation plan is reordered so the sign is estimated before any trade expression is specified.
4. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 added at rank 3, 56/100, confidence low.** The catalogue's first non-derivatives entry: cross-sectional dispersion of crypto returns as a state variable that switches off cross-sectional momentum when the cross-section disagrees most. Published, methodologically explicit prior art (Zhang and Makgolo, April 2026); the blocking constraint is a free point-in-time crypto universe, not the idea.
5. **Standing prior recorded for the next cycle:** in crypto, crowding-style signals (funding extremes, elevated basis, cross-venue premiums) have now been contradicted in three preregistered tests, all in the same direction — they accompany continuation rather than exhaustion. Any new contrarian candidate in this domain must beat that prior before it is promoted.

| Rank | ID | Thesis | Total score | Confidence |
|------|-----|--------|-------------|------------|
| 1 | CRYPTO-EDGE-001 | Extreme perp basis predicts elevated crash frequency and severity (not negative mean returns) over 1-4 weeks | 63/100 | Low-medium |
| 2 | CRYPTO-EDGE-003 | Extreme cross-venue funding dispersion predicts relative underperformance of the expensive venue over 5-20 days | 58/100 | Low |
| 3 | CRYPTO-EDGE-004 | Extreme cross-sectional dispersion breaks down the cross-sectional momentum spread over the next month | 56/100 | Low |

*(The sixth-cycle table above is preserved as history. Its CRYPTO-EDGE-001 total of 63 was an arithmetic error: the correct figure for those dimension scores is 67, and 61 is the seventh-cycle figure after re-scoring. The sixth cycle's other totals - CRYPTO-EDGE-002 at 46, CRYPTO-EDGE-003 at 58 and CRYPTO-EDGE-004 at 56 - were correct.)*

## Statement
No empirical strategy validation (backtests, paper trading, forward tests, parameter sweeps) was performed in this repository. This is research documentation only. Published and preregistered results from external sources are cited as evidence about the world, not as results of any test run here. The seventh, eighth and ninth cycles read, fetched and checked external sources directly, including public API endpoints and public data archives whose availability, coverage and access limits were verified by hand; no return, Sharpe, drawdown or performance figure for any strategy in this catalogue was computed, and no strategy was executed. In the ninth cycle the only files fetched from the Binance public data archive were directory listings and a small number of single-day kline and metrics files, and they were read **solely** to determine data availability, listing and last-trading dates, and field definitions; no return, spread, price statistic or strategy result was computed from them.

## Eighteenth Research Cycle (2026-10-20) — Summary

- **Re-derived totals:** All four re-derived from seven dimension contributions and checked against shortlist: **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 = 58** (16+6+3+15+6+8+4), **CRYPTO-EDGE-002 = 46** (rejected; 12+3+3+15+3+6+4), **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 = 55** (12+3+6+15+3+8+8), **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 = 48** (12+6+3+12+3+8+4). **No score moves.** Rank order unchanged (001 > 003 > 004, margins 3 and 7 points).
- **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 — regime gap remains permanent and supply-side.** Exhaustive census of publisher bucket confirmed no per-contract forced-liquidation record after 2024-10-14 (linear ends 2024-03-31, inverse ends 2024-10-14; `data2/` has no futures tree). A second centralised venue's `liquidation-orders` endpoint returns per-contract forced liquidations prospectively (~5h rolling window, no working pagination) — useful for live monitoring but **not retrospectively reconstructable** for the post-July-2024 / Oct 2025 cascade regime. **Transport assumption would need to be pre-registered and falsifiable** (venue class/collateral/liquidity, instrument specs, execution regime) if ever pursued; no such transport test performed. Proxy decomposition remains explicit: recorded forced liquidations explain only **~12.1%** of OI reduction on cascade days (24.8% day before, 4.9% day after), ratio unstable across contracts/days. **Falsifiability disagreement** remains: remaining obstacle is the regime gap — placement between data-access (ceiling already 5/5), falsifiability, and robustness; cycle holds position, disagreement carried.
- **CRYPTO-EDGE-003 — specification applied as written.** Pair (Binance USDⓈ-M vs OKX USDT-margined swap, 2022-01-01 to 2025-09-05) is constructible. Added to specification: pre-registered pair/collateral/venue class, **negative control reported as pre-registered prior** (centralised pair spread centred near zero, coin-flip sign/stability per source), **liquidity-filter second-leg substitute declared** (no dated OI on OKX archive; use turnover from candlesticks with asymmetry stated), **coverage limit stated** (OKX funding archive ends 2025-09-05). The key cost question remains: thresholded selection must beat unthresholded patient carry on same clock/window/cost model/inference with pre-committed redundancy null. Evidence quality stays 1/5 (no supporting evidence for registered mapping); phenomenon-level evidence runs against entry (documented losses for thresholded rule). No score change.
- **CRYPTO-EDGE-004 — size-stratum specification applied.** Free Coin Metrics community market-cap panel (104 priced assets at 2026-10-19, reaches below reference screen bottom quintile) shows small stratum is different population (deprecated tokens + 4 stablecoins, large boundary divergences vs reference). Applied clauses: **exclude stablecoins**, **apply liveness rule** (asset must have traded on date), **report free screen size composition against reference screen per date**, **cross-check market caps at stratum boundary**, **estimate unconditional spread on same population**, **compare within stratum with size-matched control**. Base effect sign-fragile by size (documented in 3 sources); null may reflect wrong population. Free-data accessibility already moved to 4/5 in previous cycle; no further move.
- **Adversarial checks.** Re-tested carried disagreements (001 falsifiability, 003 free-data direction, 004 free-data direction) — placements held. Select-versus-harvest prior reinforced with cross-sectional leg (001 proxy decomposition shows small caps see larger OI collapse and larger forced-flow share). Rejected forced-unwind cross-sectional variant remains rejected on coincident-label/cost grounds despite better coverage on linear record. No new hypothesis added (event count remains binding constraint; six candidates examined, none cleared bar).
- **No empirical validation, backtests, performance statistics, or strategy optimisation performed.** All changes are research documentation only.

## Previous cycle (twentieth research cycle)

A **measurement/specification and adversarial review cycle** building on the nineteenth cycle. No hypothesis was added. Totals re-derived from seven dimension contributions: **CRYPTO-EDGE-001 = 58, CRYPTO-EDGE-002 = 46 (rejected), CRYPTO-EDGE-003 = 55, CRYPTO-EDGE-004 = 48**. Rank order unchanged (001 > 003 > 004, margins 3 and 7). All carried disagreements reviewed and upheld.

**Key findings:**
1. **Re-derived totals** confirmed across all artefacts; no dimension moves. Three recorded disagreements carried: 001 falsifiability held at 4/5 (total 58) — regime gap remains permanent/supply-side at publisher (exhaustive bucket census) though a prospective ~5h liquidation route exists on another centralised venue (not retrospective, would require pre-registered transport assumptions); 003 free-data 4/5→5/5 (total 55) with negative control as pre-registered prior; 004 free-data 3/5→4/5 (total 48) with size-stratum specification applied on free Coin Metrics panel.
2. **CRYPTO-EDGE-001**: Regime gap remains binding. Any transport from prospective source must be pre-registered (venue/collateral/instrument, liquidity regime, execution, mapping to Binance USDⓈ-M) with explicit falsifiers. Rescope to measurable window (2023-06-25 to 2024-03-31) with coverage limits and comparator role stated. Proxy decomposition (12.1% of OI reduction explained on cascade days) retained.
3. **CRYPTO-EDGE-003**: Specification applied as written (Binance USDⓈ-M vs OKX USDT-margined swap, 2022-01-01–2025-09-05). Pre-registered negative control (centralised pair centred near zero, sign coin-flip) reported as prior; liquidity-filter substitute declared with asymmetry (no dated OI); coverage limit stated (funding ends 2025-09-05). Thresholded vs unthresholded patient carry must be tested with pre-committed redundancy null on same clock/window/cost model/inference. Evidence quality remains 1/5.
4. **CRYPTO-EDGE-004**: Size-stratum specification fully applied (exclude stablecoins, liveness rule, report composition vs reference per date, boundary cross-check, unconditional spread on same population, within-stratum comparison with size-matched control). Small stratum composition differs from reference; base effect sign-fragile by stratum/population.
5. **Adversarial**: Carried disagreements upheld; rejected forced-unwind variant retained on coincident-label/cost grounds; six candidate families examined, none cleared bar (event count binding, coincident-label/testable-prior failures). No hypothesis added.

**New this cycle:** No hypothesis added. No score moves. Adversarial review completed across all carried disagreements. All artefacts updated.

Please, migrate to the API v4") and **no data payload**; `/v4/catalog` → **HTTP 404**; no `data3/` directory exists under the workspace root or its parent; `cm_met.log`, `dl_allswap.out`, `dl_legacy.out` still record `python3: can't open file .../dl.py` / `download.py`. No re-measurement was possible, so no new numeric claim was made.
